Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the cryptography structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 304. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 424 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by cryptography generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 304, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 424. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a cryptography algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 13. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 114/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 13 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cryptography structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 114. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
